Resident Evil Resistance, Windows PC Route

Spoiler-free completion of every live mode, map, roster slot, rank gate, skill variant, match condition, weapon, equipment row, deck card, rotating mission, cosmetic entry, and final system audit · 58 areas · 1381 tracked items

This page shows everything, including exact locations. It exists so you can search for one specific collectible and find it. If you are playing blind, use the interactive spoiler-free version instead, it hides locations until you ask for them, one item at a time.

Open the spoiler-free interactive guide

PC Route and Online Status

You are here when: The Steam library entry, separate multiplayer launcher, and online main menu.

Current play is possible but population-dependent; an arranged group is the dependable route.

Windows and Steam Route

Route

HintUse the native PC release tied to the companion game.
WhereInstall the separate Resistance client from the same Steam library entitlement.
Full detailUse the native Windows client; no secondary platform or emulation pass adds exclusive content. The client needs a continuous network connection, so confirm sign-in before organizing players.

Bundled Client Access

Access

HintThe multiplayer client is attached to another library purchase.
WhereCheck the Steam library rather than searching for a separate standalone purchase.
Full detailResistance is supplied with the Windows release of Resident Evil 3. If the store page is unlisted, install it from the owned library entry; do not obtain an unofficial client.

Peer-to-Peer Service Check (missable)

Online Check

HintTest the online menu before planning a long completion session.
WhereOpen Quick Match and verify that matchmaking begins without a service error.
Full detailAs of 2026-07-15, matches still use peer hosting and no dedicated match server is required. Availability depends on other players and platform authentication, so record any new service notice before proceeding.

Arranged Five-Player Group

Online Setup

HintA full private group removes the population bottleneck.
WherePrepare four Survivor players and one Mastermind player before opening Custom Game.
Full detailUse an arranged group of five for map, skill, and match-condition checks. Rotate the solo Mastermind role after each planned set, and use Quick Match separately whenever rank, RP, or mission credit must be recorded.

Profile Evidence Backup (missable)

Backup

HintKeep a dated record of online-only progress.
WhereCapture ranks, owned equipment, deck cards, and cosmetic totals after each session.
Full detailCopy the local save and configuration, then retain dated menu and results captures. Server-side records cannot be reconstructed from the pack if online access changes later.

Final Content Revision

Version

HintMake sure every late content category appears in the menus.
WhereConfirm that all seven maps, seven Survivor slots, and five Mastermind slots are present.
Full detailUpdate to the final PC build. Official content and balance support ended in October 2020; the complete build includes the late maps, added equipment, final card set, and final cosmetic catalogue.

Tutorials, Practice, and Match Modes

You are here when: The mode selector containing Tutorial, Practice, Quick Match, and Custom Game.

Private play is best for checks; live matchmaking is used for persistent rewards.

Survivor Tutorial

Tutorial

HintLearn the team role before joining a live lobby.
WhereChoose Tutorial from the main menu and select the Survivor lesson.
Full detailComplete every prompted movement, item, objective, and exit step until the tutorial returns to the menu with a clear state.

Mastermind Tutorial

Tutorial

HintLearn the camera role before building a deck.
WhereChoose Tutorial from the main menu and select the Mastermind lesson.
Full detailComplete every camera, card, energy, control, and environmental-interaction prompt until the tutorial records completion.

Survivor Practice

Practice

HintUse the offline-style sandbox to test controls safely.
WhereOpen Practice, choose the Survivor side, and load one map.
Full detailAdjust the available practice parameters, enter the map, purchase an item, trigger both active skill types, and finish or exit only after the controls are confirmed.

Mastermind Practice

Practice

HintTest deck placement without holding up other players.
WhereOpen Practice, choose the Mastermind side, and load one map.
Full detailCycle cameras, spend bio energy, place each available card type, use an environmental control, and deploy the ultimate once before leaving practice.

Quick Match as Survivor

Mode

HintUse public matchmaking for progression that private play may not grant.
WhereFinish one Quick Match while queued as a Survivor.
Full detailSelect the Survivor role, complete the entire match without disconnecting, and confirm that rank, RP, and mission counters update on the results screen.

Quick Match as Mastermind

Mode

HintThe solo role has its own progression ledger.
WhereFinish one Quick Match while queued as the Mastermind.
Full detailSelect the Mastermind role, remain host through the result, and verify rank, RP, and active mission progress before returning to matchmaking.

Quick Match Random Queue

Mode

HintOne queue option trades role certainty for a bonus.
WhereComplete one match using the Random role option.
Full detailChoose Random under Quick Match, accept whichever role is assigned, finish normally, and confirm the role-selection RP bonus on the result.

Custom Game

Mode

HintUse a private lobby for controlled mechanical checks.
WhereCreate a Custom Game and fill all five player positions.
Full detailSet a map, assign four players to the team side and one to the camera side, complete the match, then rotate roles. Do not assume private results award rank or RP; verify those in Quick Match.

Map Preset Editor

Customization

HintThe solo role can prepare environmental layouts in advance.
WhereReach rank 5 with any Mastermind slot, then open the map-preset editor.
Full detailSave one valid preset for every map. Place the permitted objects in each area, keep within its point allowance, and load each preset once in a Custom Game to confirm it is selectable.

Downtown

You are here when: The loading card and preset selector labelled Downtown.

Both roles and the editable preset are checked without revealing match events in advance.

Downtown, Survivor Clear

Map Clear

HintLearn the supply stations and objective routes before attempting a clean result.
WhereFinish Downtown on the four-player side.
Full detailSelect Downtown in Custom Game, complete all three mechanical areas, gather every teammate at the final exit, and cross before the clock expires.

Downtown, Mastermind Clear

Map Clear

HintTest the fixed cameras and environmental controls before defending the whole layout.
WhereFinish Downtown on the solo camera side.
Full detailSelect Downtown in Custom Game, use cameras and the saved preset across all reached areas, and reduce the shared timer to zero before the team completes the final exit.

Downtown, Saved Preset

Map Preset

HintPrepare a legal layout rather than relying only on the default arrangement.
WhereCreate and save a custom preset for Downtown.
Full detailOpen the preset editor after reaching Mastermind rank 5 on any slot, configure every available area of Downtown, save without an allowance error, then load that preset in Custom Game.

Survivor Loadout Slot #1

You are here when: The numbered Survivor slot at position 1 in the team-side Customize grid.

Roster identities stay hidden; rank, loadout, and skill checks use menu positions only.

Survivor Slot #1, All Skills

Rank

HintKeep this loadout in the live rotation until every variant opens.
WhereRaise Survivor Slot #1 to rank 20.
Full detailFinish Quick Matches with Survivor Slot #1 until rank 20 is recorded. Open all four skill families and verify that every final variant is selectable.

Survivor Slot #1, Rank Appearance

Appearance

HintA long-term appearance waits beyond the skill cap.
WhereRaise Survivor Slot #1 to rank 50.
Full detailContinue completed Quick Matches with Survivor Slot #1 through rank 50, then open Customize and confirm the gold appearance is owned and can be equipped.

Survivor Slot #1, Full-Team Escape

Roster Mastery

HintProtect all four players through the final gathering point.
WhereComplete a match with the whole team escaping while using Survivor Slot #1.
Full detailUse an arranged match, keep all four players active, revive anyone who is downed, gather everyone inside the final exit boundary, and cross together before time expires.

Survivor Slot #1, Four-Item Build

Loadout

HintTest a full equipment setup after opening the shared catalogue.
WhereEquip four distinct items on Survivor Slot #1 without exceeding its point limit.
Full detailOpen Customize, fill all four equipment positions for Survivor Slot #1, stay within the displayed point allowance, save the build, and finish one match with it active.

Survivor Slot #1, Fever Variant #1

Skill Variant

HintTry another option in this loadout's skill grid.
WhereOpen Survivor Slot #1, choose Fever variant 1, and save the loadout.
Full detailReach rank 20 with Survivor Slot #1. In Customize, open the Fever family, select variant 1 from left to right, then complete a match with that variant equipped and trigger it when the skill permits.

Deck Cards, Creatures

You are here when: The Creatures group inside Mastermind deck customization.

Names that could expose creature or ultimate identities are replaced by stable category positions.

Creatures Card #1

Deck Card

HintA different card changes the solo role's next placement or effect.
WhereOpen Customize, enter Deck Cards, choose Creatures, and inspect row 1; its displayed energy cost is 0.
Full detailThis row is available from the initial deck catalogue. Add Creatures row 1 to a legal ten-card deck, use it during a match when its placement conditions are valid, and finish the match.

Full detailRaise Mastermind Slot 5 to rank 18 in Quick Match. Add Creatures row 18 to a legal ten-card deck, use it during a match when its placement conditions are valid, and finish the match.

Survivor Appearance

Survivor Appearance

HintAnother optional customization entry belongs to this catalogue.

If the row is not in the chest pool, verify its platform in-game-item entitlement instead of spending more RP. Equip or play the unlocked entry once, then retain the owned-state capture.

Final System Audit

You are here when: The supply marker during matches and the completed Customize catalogues afterward.

The closing audit covers shipped systems only; absent campaign structures are not padded.

Supply Target, Survivor Recovery

Match System

HintWatch the map when a marked supply carrier appears.
WhereDefeat the marked supply carrier before the solo player protects it.
Full detailFollow the map marker, defeat the supply carrier, collect its dropped special item, use that item before the match ends, and confirm any active mission counter.

Supply Target, Mastermind Protection

Match System

HintA temporary marked unit can reward the solo side if kept active.
WhereProtect one marked supply carrier until its reward becomes available.
Full detailTrack the marked carrier through cameras, lock nearby doors, deploy cards along the approach, prevent its defeat, then use the awarded special card before finishing the match.

Cosmetic Chest and Claim Check

Shop

HintSave appearance spending until the equipment catalogue is stable.
WhereEarn or spend 50,000 RP for one Cosmetic Chest and open it.
Full detailComplete the active mission claims or use earned RP, open one 50,000-RP Cosmetic Chest, note the random category and row, then confirm the same row is marked owned in Customize.

RP Booster and Usage History

Shop

HintA paid multiplier is optional and should never replace normal completion.
WhereOpen the RP Booster shop and its usage-history panel.
Full detailIf a lawful booster entitlement already exists, enable one before Quick Match, finish the match, and verify its consumption in usage history. Do not buy a booster merely to mark this optional system complete.

Survivor Low-Tier Equipment Chests

Shop

HintBegin with the least expensive team-side equipment pool.
WhereOpen the low-tier Survivor pool at 3,000 RP per chest; the preserved baseline contains 35.
Full detailUse earned RP in Shop > Equipment Chest > Survivor > Low. Continue beyond the 35-row baseline if the final client still shows unowned late additions, and stop only when that live tier is exhausted.

Survivor Mid-Tier Equipment Chests

Shop

HintMove to the middle team-side pool after the low tier is empty.
WhereOpen the mid-tier Survivor pool at 8,000 RP per chest; the preserved baseline contains 31.
Full detailUse earned RP in Shop > Equipment Chest > Survivor > Mid. Continue beyond the 31-row baseline if the final client shows a late addition, and stop only when no mid-tier row remains locked.

Survivor High-Tier Equipment Chests

Shop

HintFinish the most expensive team-side pool last.
WhereOpen the high-tier Survivor pool at 20,000 RP per chest; the preserved baseline contains 33.
Full detailUse earned RP in Shop > Equipment Chest > Survivor > High. Continue beyond the 33-row baseline if the final client shows a late addition, and stop only when no high-tier row remains locked.

All Equipment Owned

Catalogue Audit

HintFinish the chest catalogue before spending RP only on appearances.
WhereConfirm every Survivor and Mastermind equipment row is marked owned.
Full detailCompare all eight equipment category tabs against this pack's row counts. Purchase the remaining chest tiers, clear every unowned mark, and save one legal four-item build for each roster slot.

All Deck Cards Owned

Catalogue Audit

HintFinish every rank gate before finalizing solo-role decks.
WhereConfirm every standard and exclusive card row is selectable.
Full detailReach rank 20 on all five Mastermind slots, inspect every card category, clear all rank locks, and use each row once across saved decks and arranged matches.

All Obtainable Cosmetics Owned

Catalogue Audit

HintUse the category totals rather than trusting a single chest counter.
WhereConfirm every currently obtainable customization row is owned or documented as entitlement-only.
